```go
	}
	log.Debug("dial ok", "server", srv.Name, "addr", addr,
		"dur", time.Since(dialStart).Round(time.Millisecond))
	_ = conn.SetDeadline(dl) // bounds handshake + all later session I/O; a no-op on a tunnel, whose hop socket carries dl
	// Cancellation (Ctrl-C) must abort a handshake in flight, not wait for
	// the deadline: closing the socket unblocks every read. Closing a tunnel
	// only sends a close and waits for the peer's reply, so the hop is
	// closed too (spec S5, fact 4).
	stopCancel := context.AfterFunc(ctx, func() { _ = conn.Close(); hop.closeQuiet() })
	defer stopCancel()

		User:              srv.User,
		Auth:              []ssh.AuthMethod{auth},
		HostKeyCallback:   cb,
		HostKeyAlgorithms: kh.HostKeyAlgorithms(addr),
	})
	if err != nil {
		_ = conn.Close()
		hop.closeQuiet()
		return nil, dialFacts{user: srv.User, addr: addr, hk: hk, rec: rec, asked: len(opts.Only), missing: missing, offer: offer}.failure(ctx, err)
	}
	if !stopCancel() {
		// ctx ended exactly as the handshake completed: the socket is closed.
		_ = sshConn.Close()
		hop.closeQuiet()
		return nil, classify(ctxKind(ctx), fmt.Errorf("ssh %s@%s: %w", srv.User, addr, ctx.Err()))
	}
	c := &Conn{Client: ssh.NewClient(sshConn, chans, reqs), session: rec, hop: hop, raw: conn}
	if opts.SessionTimeout > 0 {
		c.setDeadline(time.Now().Add(opts.SessionTimeout))
	}
	log.Debug("auth ok", "server", srv.Name, "user", srv.User)
	return c, nil
```

Extend `ConnectWith`'s doc comment with: `A via server is reached through a fresh connection to its bastion (dialTarget): the hop gets the same ctx, offers keys as any connection to that bastion would, and is closed with the target and on every failure.`

Add the dial helpers after `dialErr` (`:332`):

```go
// dialTarget opens srv's transport: a TCP dial, or for a via server a
// direct-tcpip channel through a fresh connection to its bastion (one hop
// connection per target, never pooled: the hop's socket carries this
// target's deadline). hop is nil for a direct dial. A via the Dialer cannot
// resolve fails closed: srv is never dialed directly.
func (d *Dialer) dialTarget(ctx context.Context, srv config.Server, addr string) (net.Conn, *Conn, error) {
	if srv.Via == "" {
		dialer := net.Dialer{Timeout: d.DialTimeout}
		conn, err := dialer.DialContext(ctx, "tcp", addr)
		if err != nil {
			return nil, nil, dialFailure(ctx, addr, err)
		}
		return conn, nil, nil
	}
	hopSrv, ok := d.lookup(srv.Via)
	if !ok {
		return nil, nil, classify(fleet.ErrorKindDial, fmt.Errorf("%s: via %q is not a server this run knows — refusing to dial %s directly", srv.Name, srv.Via, addr))
	}
	hop, err := d.ConnectWith(ctx, hopSrv, ConnectOptions{}) // never Only or Prefer: the hop offers as any connection to it does
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, viaFailure(hopSrv.Name, err)
	}
	conn, err := hop.Client.DialContext(ctx, "tcp", addr)
	if err != nil {
		_ = hop.Close()
		return nil, nil, forwardFailure(ctx, hopSrv.Name, addr, err)
	}
	return conn, hop, nil
}

// lookup resolves a via name through Dialer.Server; false without one.
func (d *Dialer) lookup(name string) (config.Server, bool) {
	if d.Server == nil {
		return config.Server{}, false
	}
	return d.Server(name)
}

// route lists the bastions srv is reached through, root first, as far as
// the Dialer resolves them; nil for a direct server.
func (d *Dialer) route(srv config.Server) []config.Server {
	var out []config.Server
	for v := srv.Via; v != "" && len(out) <= config.MaxViaDepth; {
		hop, ok := d.lookup(v)
		if !ok {
			break
		}
		out = append(out, hop)
		v = hop.Via
	}
	slices.Reverse(out)
	return out
}

// viaFailure is a dependent's error when its bastion could not be reached:
// the hop's kind and hint, "via NAME: " before the hop's own text, and the
// failing bastion as HopStage — the innermost one of a longer chain. The
// MaxAuthTries signal is not carried: it is the bastion's, not the
// server's.
func viaFailure(hop string, err error) error {
	stage := hop
	var e *Error
	if errors.As(err, &e) && e.HopStage != "" {
		stage = e.HopStage
	}
	return &Error{Kind: fleet.ErrorKindOf(err), Err: fmt.Errorf("via %s: %w", hop, err), Hint: fleet.HintOf(err), HopStage: stage}
}

// forwardFailure classifies a direct-tcpip channel the bastion did not open
// (gap item 2). Prohibited (AllowTcpForwarding no, no-port-forwarding,
// restrict, permitopen=) is the bastion's doing: kind dial, ForwardHint,
// HopStage set. Any other rejection means the bastion could not reach the
// server: the server's own dial failure, with HopDialHint (DialHint's
// "needs via:" would be wrong advice here). Server text is bounded
// (remoteText).
func forwardFailure(ctx context.Context, hop, addr string, err error) error {
	if ctx.Err() != nil {
		return classify(ctxKind(ctx), fmt.Errorf("via %s: open %s: %w", hop, addr, ctx.Err()))
	}
	var oce *ssh.OpenChannelError
	if errors.As(err, &oce) && oce.Reason == ssh.Prohibited {
		return &Error{Kind: fleet.ErrorKindDial, Hint: ForwardHint, HopStage: hop,
			Err: fmt.Errorf("via %s: forward to %s refused: %w", hop, addr, remoteText{err})}
	}
	return classifyHint(fleet.ErrorKindDial, HopDialHint, fmt.Errorf("via %s: %w", hop, dialErr(addr, remoteText{err})))
}
```

Replace `keyscanCompareCmd` (`:104-112`) with:

```go
// keyscanCompareCmd builds the fingerprint-comparison pipeline: scanned
// keys go to ssh-keygen -lf - for display, never into the trust store. For
// a server behind bastions (route, root first) the scan runs on its own
// bastion, since the operator's machine cannot reach it, and is labelled as
// that bastion's view; outer bastions go in one -J.
func keyscanCompareCmd(presented ssh.PublicKey, srv config.Server, route []config.Server) string {
	typ := ""
	if presented != nil {
		typ = "-t " + keyscanType(presented.Type()) + " " // x/crypto-parsed type: a fixed algorithm name
	}
	if len(route) == 0 {
		return "ssh-keyscan " + typ + "-p " + strconv.Itoa(srv.Port) + " " + ShQuote(srv.Host) + " | ssh-keygen -lf -"
	}
	hop := route[len(route)-1]
	jump := ""
	if outer := route[:len(route)-1]; len(outer) > 0 {
		hops := make([]string, 0, len(outer))
		for _, h := range outer {
			hops = append(hops, h.User+"@"+net.JoinHostPort(h.Host, strconv.Itoa(h.Port)))
		}
		jump = "-J " + localWord(strings.Join(hops, ",")) + " "
	}
	return "ssh " + jump + "-p " + strconv.Itoa(hop.Port) + " " + localWord(hop.User+"@"+hop.Host) +
		" ssh-keyscan " + typ + "-p " + strconv.Itoa(srv.Port) + " " + remoteWord(srv.Host) +
		" | ssh-keygen -lf - (as seen by the bastion; still compare with the console)"
}

// plainWord is what every shell reads as one literal word: no globs, no
// quotes, no expansions.
var plainWord = regexp.MustCompile(`^[A-Za-z0-9._:@,-]+$`)

// localWord is s as one word for the operator's shell.
func localWord(s string) string {
	if plainWord.MatchString(s) {
		return s
	}
	return ShQuote(s)
}

// remoteWord is s as one word for the bastion's shell too: ssh joins its
// arguments into one command line the far side parses again, so a word
// that is not plain is quoted twice (gap item 5).
func remoteWord(s string) string {
	if plainWord.MatchString(s) {
		return s
	}
	return ShQuote(ShQuote(s))
}
```

```go
	target := sshtest.NewServer(t, sshtest.ServerConfig{HostKeys: []ssh.Signer{targetKey}, Authorized: []ssh.PublicKey{user.PublicKey()}, Handle: sshtest.ShellHandler(home)})
```

```go
package sshwrite

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"net"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"golang.org/x/crypto/ssh"

	"github.com/javadh75/SSHepherd/internal/authkeys"
	"github.com/javadh75/SSHepherd/internal/config"
	"github.com/javadh75/SSHepherd/internal/fleet"
	"github.com/javadh75/SSHepherd/internal/sshconn"
	"github.com/javadh75/SSHepherd/internal/sshconn/sshtest"
)

// TestClassifyProbeHopStage: a failure at a bastion is "could not check"
// whatever its kind (spec S5): never a rejection, so never a restore.
func TestClassifyProbeHopStage(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tt := range []struct {
		name  string
		stage probeStage
		err   error
		want  verdict
	}{
		{"hop refused the key", stageDial, &sshconn.Error{Kind: fleet.ErrorKindAuth, HopStage: "b", Err: errors.New("x")}, verdictTransient},
		{"hop MaxAuthTries", stageDial, &sshconn.Error{Kind: fleet.ErrorKindHandshake, HopStage: "b", AuthExhausted: true, Err: errors.New("x")}, verdictTransient},
		{"hop host key changed", stageDial, &sshconn.Error{Kind: fleet.ErrorKindHostKeyChanged, HopStage: "b", Err: errors.New("x")}, verdictHostKey},
		{"forward prohibited at the hop", stageDial, &sshconn.Error{Kind: fleet.ErrorKindDial, HopStage: "b", Err: errors.New("x")}, verdictTransient},
		{"the server refused the key", stageDial, &sshconn.Error{Kind: fleet.ErrorKindAuth, Err: errors.New("x")}, verdictRejected},
	} {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			if got := classifyProbe(tt.stage, tt.err); got != tt.want {
				t.Errorf("classifyProbe = %v, want %v", got, tt.want)
			}
		})
	}
}

// probeHome seeds a temp HOME whose authorized_keys holds line.
func probeHome(t *testing.T, line string) string {
	t.Helper()
	home, err := os.MkdirTemp("", "home")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	t.Cleanup(func() { _ = os.RemoveAll(home) })
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(home, ".ssh"), 0o700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(home, ".ssh", "authorized_keys"), []byte(line+"\n"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return home
}

// bastionWrite writes a fresh render to a server that is a bastion, with
// the given Forward, and returns Apply's error and the file afterwards.
func bastionWrite(t *testing.T, forward func(string, uint32) (net.Conn, ssh.RejectionReason)) (before, after string, err error) {
	t.Helper()
	user := sshtest.Signer(t, sshtest.Key(1))
	line := strings.TrimSpace(string(ssh.MarshalAuthorizedKey(user.PublicKey())))
	home := probeHome(t, line+"\n"+strings.TrimSpace(string(ssh.MarshalAuthorizedKey(sshtest.Signer(t, sshtest.Key(7)).PublicKey()))))
	file := filepath.Join(home, ".ssh", "authorized_keys")
	orig, rerr := os.ReadFile(file)
	if rerr != nil {
		t.Fatal(rerr)
	}
	hostKey := sshtest.Signer(t, sshtest.Key(40))
	srv := sshtest.NewServer(t, sshtest.ServerConfig{HostKeys: []ssh.Signer{hostKey}, AuthorizedKeysFile: file,
		Handle: sshtest.ShellHandler(home), Forward: forward})
	d := &sshconn.Dialer{KnownHostsPath: sshtest.KnownHostsFile(t, srv.KnownHostsLine(hostKey.PublicKey())),
		Creds: &sshconn.Credentials{AgentSock: sshtest.Agent(t, sshtest.Key(1))}, DialTimeout: 5 * time.Second}
	w := &FleetWriter{Dialer: d, sleep: func(context.Context, time.Duration) error { return nil }}
	k, _ := authkeys.ParseLine(line)
	content := authkeys.ManagedHeader + "\n" + line + "\n"
	err = w.Apply(context.Background(), config.Server{Name: "bastion", Host: srv.Host, Port: srv.Port, User: "jump"}, Request{
		Content: []byte(content), Expect: SnapshotOf(false, orig), Survivors: []string{k.Fingerprint},
		ProbeForward: "10.0.1.5:22",
	})
	now, rerr := os.ReadFile(file)
	if rerr != nil {
		t.Fatal(rerr)
	}
	return string(orig), string(now), err
}

// TestForwardProbeProhibitedRestores: a bastion whose new file may not
// forward fails the login check after a successful login: restore.
func TestForwardProbeProhibitedRestores(t *testing.T) {
	before, after, err := bastionWrite(t, sshtest.ProhibitForward)
	var lockout *fleet.LockoutError
	if !errors.As(err, &lockout) || lockout.Restore != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Apply = %v, want a restored lockout", err)
	}
	if after != before {
		t.Errorf("file after the rejected forward = %q, want the original back", after)
	}
}

// TestForwardProbeTargetDownKeepsTheFile: a dependent the bastion cannot
// reach is the dependent's problem; the write stands.
func TestForwardProbeTargetDownKeepsTheFile(t *testing.T) {
	down := func(string, uint32) (net.Conn, ssh.RejectionReason) { return nil, ssh.ConnectionFailed }
	before, after, err := bastionWrite(t, down)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Apply = %v, want success", err)
	}
	if after == before {
		t.Error("the write did not land")
	}
}

// TestProbeHopDeathIsUnproven: after the write's own connection the
// bastion stops accepting the key, so every login check fails at the hop
// with kind auth. That proves nothing about this server's file: UNPROVEN,
// kept, never a restore or a lockout (spec S5, P08). Without classifyProbe's
// hop branch the auth kind reads as verdictRejected and the file is
// restored, so this test fails before Step 3.
func TestProbeHopDeathIsUnproven(t *testing.T) {
	user := sshtest.Signer(t, sshtest.Key(1))
	line := strings.TrimSpace(string(ssh.MarshalAuthorizedKey(user.PublicKey())))
	home := probeHome(t, line)
	file := filepath.Join(home, ".ssh", "authorized_keys")
	before, err := os.ReadFile(file)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	hopFile := filepath.Join(t.TempDir(), "hop_authorized_keys")
	if err := os.WriteFile(hopFile, []byte(line+"\n"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	hopKey, targetKey := sshtest.Signer(t, sshtest.Key(40)), sshtest.Signer(t, sshtest.Key(41))
	hop := sshtest.NewServer(t, sshtest.ServerConfig{HostKeys: []ssh.Signer{hopKey}, AuthorizedKeysFile: hopFile, Forward: sshtest.DialForward})
	target := sshtest.NewServer(t, sshtest.ServerConfig{HostKeys: []ssh.Signer{targetKey}, AuthorizedKeysFile: file, Handle: sshtest.ShellHandler(home)})
	bastion := config.Server{Name: "bastion", Host: hop.Host, Port: hop.Port, User: "jump", JumpOnly: true}
	d := &sshconn.Dialer{KnownHostsPath: sshtest.KnownHostsFile(t, hop.KnownHostsLine(hopKey.PublicKey()), target.KnownHostsLine(targetKey.PublicKey())),
		Creds: &sshconn.Credentials{AgentSock: sshtest.Agent(t, sshtest.Key(1))}, DialTimeout: 5 * time.Second,
		Server: func(name string) (config.Server, bool) { return bastion, name == bastion.Name }}
	// The first connect is the write's own; from the second (the first
	// login check) on, the bastion's file is empty, so the hop refuses the
	// key (sshtest re-reads AuthorizedKeysFile on every login).
	calls := 0
	connect := func(ctx context.Context, srv config.Server, opts sshconn.ConnectOptions) (*sshconn.Conn, error) {
		calls++
		if calls == 2 {
			if err := os.WriteFile(hopFile, nil, 0o600); err != nil {
				t.Error(err)
			}
		}
		return d.ConnectWith(ctx, srv, opts)
	}
	w := &FleetWriter{Dialer: d, connect: connect, sleep: func(context.Context, time.Duration) error { return nil }}
	k, _ := authkeys.ParseLine(line)
	content := authkeys.ManagedHeader + "\n" + line + "\n"
	err = w.Apply(context.Background(), config.Server{Name: "web-1", Host: target.Host, Port: target.Port, User: "deploy", Via: "bastion"},
		Request{Content: []byte(content), Expect: SnapshotOf(false, before), Survivors: []string{k.Fingerprint}})
	var unproven *fleet.UnprovenError
	var lockout *fleet.LockoutError
	if !errors.As(err, &unproven) || errors.As(err, &lockout) || fleet.FailedHopOf(err) != "bastion" {
		t.Fatalf("Apply = %v, want UNPROVEN at the bastion and no lockout", err)
	}
	if after, _ := os.ReadFile(file); string(after) != content {
		t.Errorf("file = %q, want the new file kept", after)
	}
}
```

Replace `probeOnce` (`:108-131`) with:

```go
// probeOnce logs in offering only the given keys and runs probeCmd: a key
// that authenticates but whose line forces a command would leave sshepherd
// unable to manage the server, so the command must run and print
// probeOutput, not just a handshake. On a bastion (forward set) the same
// session then opens a direct-tcpip channel to one dependent (spec S5). The
// dial is bounded by bound, the exec and the forward by probeExecDeadline
// (which starts when the connect returns).
func (w *FleetWriter) probeOnce(ctx context.Context, srv config.Server, only []string, forward string, bound time.Duration) (probeStage, error) {
	dialCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(ctx, bound)
	defer cancel()
	probe, err := w.connectFn()(dialCtx, srv, sshconn.ConnectOptions{Only: only, SessionTimeout: probeExecDeadline})
	if err != nil {
		return stageDial, err
	}
	defer func() { _ = probe.Close() }() // errcheck: the verdict is already decided
	execCtx, cancelExec := context.WithTimeout(ctx, probeExecDeadline)
	defer cancelExec()
	code, stdout, _, err := w.execFn()(execCtx, probe.Client, probeCmd, nil)
	switch {
	case err != nil:
		return stageAuthed, fmt.Errorf("probe command: %w", err)
	case code != 0 || stdout.String() != probeOutput:
		return stageAuthed, fmt.Errorf("probe command did not run as sent (exit %d) — a forced command= on the surviving key?", code)
	case forward != "":
		return stageAuthed, w.forwardFn()(execCtx, probe.Client, forward)
	}
	return stageAuthed, nil
}

// probeForward opens a direct-tcpip channel to addr over the probe's
// connection and closes it at once: a bastion's surviving keys must still
// forward to its dependents. Only a prohibited channel fails the check (it
// follows a successful login, so it restores); a dependent that is down or
// unreachable from the bastion is the dependent's own problem.
func probeForward(ctx context.Context, client *ssh.Client, addr string) error {
	conn, err := client.DialContext(ctx, "tcp", addr)
	if err == nil {
		_ = conn.Close()
		return nil
	}
	var oce *ssh.OpenChannelError
	if errors.As(err, &oce) && oce.Reason == ssh.Prohibited {
		return fmt.Errorf("forward probe: the surviving key may not forward to %s (%w) — the servers behind this bastion would be cut off", addr, sshconn.SanitizedError{Err: err})
	}
	return nil
}
```

Add `"golang.org/x/crypto/ssh"` to `probe.go`'s imports. In `probeWithRetries` (`:163`) pass `s.req.ProbeForward` as the new fourth argument; in `headlessProof` (`:208`) pass `""` (the headless proof never restores, so it never probes the forward).

In `FleetWriter` (`internal/sshwrite/sshwrite.go:352-367`) add the seam `forward func(ctx context.Context, client *ssh.Client, addr string) error` beside `exec`, and after `execFn`:

```go
// forwardFn resolves the forward-probe seam to probeForward.
func (w *FleetWriter) forwardFn() func(context.Context, *ssh.Client, string) error {
	if w.forward != nil {
		return w.forward
	}
	return probeForward
}
```

```go
package audit

import (
	"bytes"
	"context"
	"fmt"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"sync/atomic"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/javadh75/SSHepherd/internal/config"
	"github.com/javadh75/SSHepherd/internal/fleet"
	"github.com/javadh75/SSHepherd/internal/testkeys"
)

// bastionManifest: alice (k1) granted every server; edge is a jump_only
// bastion, n servers sit behind it, and db-1 is direct.
func bastionManifest(t *testing.T, n int) *config.Config {
	t.Helper()
	var b strings.Builder
	b.WriteString("users:\n  - {name: alice, keys: [\"" + testkeys.Line(t, 1) + "\"]}\nservers:\n")
	b.WriteString("  - {name: edge, host: edge.example.com, user: jump, jump_only: true}\n")
	for i := range n {
		fmt.Fprintf(&b, "  - {name: web-%02d, host: 10.0.1.%d, user: deploy, via: edge}\n", i+1, i+1)
	}
	b.WriteString("  - {name: db-1, host: 10.0.2.1, user: deploy}\naccess:\n  - {user: alice, servers: [\"*\"]}\n")
	cfg, err := config.Parse([]byte(b.String()))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return cfg
}

// gaugeReader holds each read for hold (honoring ctx) and records the most
// reads in flight through a bastion at once.
type gaugeReader struct {
	hold      time.Duration
	now, peak atomic.Int32
	mu        sync.Mutex
	read      []string
	content   []byte
}

func (g *gaugeReader) ReadAuthorizedKeys(ctx context.Context, srv config.Server) (fleet.ReadResult, error) {
	g.mu.Lock()
	g.read = append(g.read, srv.Name)
	g.mu.Unlock()
	if srv.Via != "" {
		n := g.now.Add(1)
		defer g.now.Add(-1)
		for { // raise peak to n (revive: no empty loop body)
			p := g.peak.Load()
			if n <= p || g.peak.CompareAndSwap(p, n) {
				break
			}
		}
	}
	select {
	case <-time.After(g.hold):
		return fleet.ReadResult{Content: g.content}, nil
	case <-ctx.Done():
		return fleet.ReadResult{}, fmt.Errorf("read %s: %w", srv.Name, ctx.Err())
	}
}

// TestRunNeverReadsJumpOnly: the jump_only row is added without a reader
// call or a fan-out slot, and is no error.
func TestRunNeverReadsJumpOnly(t *testing.T) {
	cfg := bastionManifest(t, 1)
	g := &gaugeReader{}
	results := Run(context.Background(), cfg, g, Options{Parallel: 1})
	if len(results) != 3 || results[1].Server.Name != "edge" || results[1].Err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("results = %+v, want edge listed with no error", results)
	}
	for _, name := range g.read {
		if name == "edge" {
			t.Fatal("the jump_only server was read")
		}
	}
}

// TestRunCapsReadsPerBastion (OP2): never more than fleet.HopCap reads in
// flight through one bastion, whatever --parallel says.
func TestRunCapsReadsPerBastion(t *testing.T) {
	g := &gaugeReader{hold: 30 * time.Millisecond}
	Run(context.Background(), bastionManifest(t, 3*fleet.HopCap), g, Options{Parallel: 32})
	if p := g.peak.Load(); p > fleet.HopCap || p < 2 {
		t.Errorf("peak reads through edge = %d, want 2..%d", p, fleet.HopCap)
	}
}

// TestRunCapsReadsThroughWholeChain: a read behind mid1 or mid2 takes a
// slot on edge too (its whole chain, root first), so the reads in flight
// through edge stay at fleet.HopCap however they split between the inner
// bastions. Every server here except edge and db-1 sits behind edge, so the
// gauge's "has a via" count is the count through edge.
func TestRunCapsReadsThroughWholeChain(t *testing.T) {
	var b strings.Builder
	b.WriteString("users:\n  - {name: alice, keys: [\"" + testkeys.Line(t, 1) + "\"]}\nservers:\n")
	b.WriteString("  - {name: edge, host: edge.example.com, user: jump, jump_only: true}\n")
	b.WriteString("  - {name: mid1, host: 10.0.8.1, user: ops, via: edge}\n  - {name: mid2, host: 10.0.9.1, user: ops, via: edge}\n")
	for i := range fleet.HopCap {
		fmt.Fprintf(&b, "  - {name: a-%02d, host: 10.0.1.%d, user: deploy, via: mid1}\n", i+1, i+1)
		fmt.Fprintf(&b, "  - {name: b-%02d, host: 10.0.2.%d, user: deploy, via: mid2}\n", i+1, i+1)
	}
	b.WriteString("  - {name: db-1, host: 10.0.3.1, user: deploy}\naccess:\n  - {user: alice, servers: [\"*\"]}\n")
	cfg, err := config.Parse([]byte(b.String()))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	g := &gaugeReader{hold: 30 * time.Millisecond}
	Run(context.Background(), cfg, g, Options{Parallel: 64})
	if p := g.peak.Load(); p > fleet.HopCap {
		t.Errorf("peak reads through edge = %d, want at most %d (a read takes a slot on every bastion of its chain)", p, fleet.HopCap)
	}
}

// TestRunBastionWaitBurnsNoBudget: the slot is taken before the per-server
// timeout starts, so a server queued behind a full bastion still gets its
// whole budget. It passes before Step 3 too (no limiter: all 17 reads run
// at once inside 250ms); it pins the slot-before-budget order against
// mutant 16, and TestRunCapsReadsPerBastion is the failing-first test.
func TestRunBastionWaitBurnsNoBudget(t *testing.T) {
	g := &gaugeReader{hold: 150 * time.Millisecond}
	for _, r := range Run(context.Background(), bastionManifest(t, 2*fleet.HopCap), g, Options{Parallel: 32, PerServerTimeout: 250 * time.Millisecond}) {
		if r.Err != nil {
			t.Errorf("%s: %v (the wait for a bastion slot used its budget)", r.Server.Name, r.Err)
		}
	}
}

// TestRunCancelWhileWaitingForBastion: Ctrl-C releases a server still
// queued behind a full bastion without reading it.
func TestRunCancelWhileWaitingForBastion(t *testing.T) {
	g := &gaugeReader{hold: time.Hour}
	ctx, cancel := context.WithCancel(context.Background())
	time.AfterFunc(100*time.Millisecond, cancel)
	results := Run(ctx, bastionManifest(t, fleet.HopCap+1), g, Options{Parallel: 32})
	if n := len(g.read); n != fleet.HopCap+1 { // HopCap through edge, plus db-1
		t.Errorf("reads started = %d, want %d", n, fleet.HopCap+1)
	}
	for _, r := range results {
		if r.Server.JumpOnly {
			continue
		}
		if k := fleet.ErrorKindOf(r.Err); k != fleet.ErrorKindCanceled {
			t.Errorf("%s: kind %q, want canceled", r.Server.Name, k)
		}
	}
}

// hopFail is a transport-like error at a bastion (audit cannot import
// sshconn, ARC-8).
type hopFail struct {
	kind fleet.ErrorKind
	hop  string
}

func (e hopFail) Error() string              { return "via " + e.hop + ": presented key changed" }
func (e hopFail) ErrorKind() fleet.ErrorKind { return e.kind }
func (e hopFail) FailedHop() string          { return e.hop }
func (e hopFail) ErrorHint() string {
	return "HOST KEY CHANGED for edge.example.com:22 — possible impersonation"
}

// scriptedReader serves content to every server, except the ones errs
// fails.
type scriptedReader struct {
	content []byte
	errs    map[string]error
}

func (s scriptedReader) ReadAuthorizedKeys(_ context.Context, srv config.Server) (fleet.ReadResult, error) {
	if err := s.errs[srv.Name]; err != nil {
		return fleet.ReadResult{}, err
	}
	return fleet.ReadResult{Content: s.content}, nil
}

// renderBoth checks the text and JSON reports against name's goldens
// (checkGolden, internal/audit/report_test.go:24, reads testdata/NAME).
func renderBoth(t *testing.T, cfg *config.Config, results []ServerResult, name string) {
	t.Helper()
	var text, js bytes.Buffer
	Render(&text, cfg, results, RenderOptions{})
	if err := RenderJSON(&js, cfg, results, JSONOptions{}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	checkGolden(t, name+".golden", text.Bytes())
	checkGolden(t, name+".golden.json", js.Bytes())
}

// TestRenderJumpOnlyGolden: the jump only row, the via head, status
// jump_only, summary.jump_only, exit 0.
func TestRenderJumpOnlyGolden(t *testing.T) {
	cfg := bastionManifest(t, 1)
	results := Run(context.Background(), cfg, scriptedReader{content: []byte(testkeys.Line(t, 1) + "\n")}, Options{Parallel: 4})
	if code := ExitCode(cfg, results); code != 0 {
		t.Errorf("ExitCode = %d, want 0", code)
	}
	renderBoth(t, cfg, results, "report_jump_only")
}

// TestRenderViaFailedGolden: one changed bastion key behind two servers is
// one host-key alert, exit 2, via_failed on each.
func TestRenderViaFailedGolden(t *testing.T) {
	cfg := bastionManifest(t, 2)
	changed := hopFail{kind: fleet.ErrorKindHostKeyChanged, hop: "edge"}
	results := Run(context.Background(), cfg, scriptedReader{content: []byte(testkeys.Line(t, 1) + "\n"),
		errs: map[string]error{"web-01": changed, "web-02": changed}}, Options{Parallel: 4})
	if code := ExitCode(cfg, results); code != 2 {
		t.Errorf("ExitCode = %d, want 2", code)
	}
	if c := tallyResults(cfg, results); c.hostKeyAlerts != 1 || c.jumpOnly != 1 {
		t.Errorf("tally = %+v, want 1 alert and 1 jump only", c)
	}
	renderBoth(t, cfg, results, "report_via_failed")
}

// TestUnknownBastionKeyIsNoAlert: an unrecorded bastion key stops the
// tunnel (unreachable, exit 1) but is no host-key alert: nothing changed,
// so nobody is paged (D28).
func TestUnknownBastionKeyIsNoAlert(t *testing.T) {
	cfg := bastionManifest(t, 2)
	unknown := hopFail{kind: fleet.ErrorKindHostKeyUnknown, hop: "edge"}
	results := Run(context.Background(), cfg, scriptedReader{content: []byte(testkeys.Line(t, 1) + "\n"),
		errs: map[string]error{"web-01": unknown, "web-02": unknown}}, Options{Parallel: 4})
	if code := ExitCode(cfg, results); code != 1 {
		t.Errorf("ExitCode = %d, want 1", code)
	}

		t.Errorf("tally = %+v, want no alert and 2 unreachable", c)
	}
}

// TestHostKeyAlertsDedupeManagedBastion: a managed bastion's own changed
// key and the same failure on every server behind it are one alert
// (alertSource keys both by the bastion's name).
func TestHostKeyAlertsDedupeManagedBastion(t *testing.T) {
	cfg, err := config.Parse([]byte("users:\n  - {name: alice, keys: [\"" + testkeys.Line(t, 1) + "\"]}\nservers:\n" +
		"  - {name: edge, host: edge.example.com, user: jump}\n" +
		"  - {name: web-01, host: 10.0.1.1, user: deploy, via: edge}\n  - {name: web-02, host: 10.0.1.2, user: deploy, via: edge}\n" +
		"access:\n  - {user: alice, servers: [\"*\"]}\n"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	own := hopFail{kind: fleet.ErrorKindHostKeyChanged} // no FailedHop: the bastion's own row
	behind := hopFail{kind: fleet.ErrorKindHostKeyChanged, hop: "edge"}
	results := Run(context.Background(), cfg, scriptedReader{errs: map[string]error{"edge": own, "web-01": behind, "web-02": behind}}, Options{Parallel: 4})
	if c := tallyResults(cfg, results); c.hostKeyAlerts != 1 || c.unreachable != 3 {
		t.Errorf("tally = %+v, want 1 alert over 3 unreachable", c)
	}
	if code := ExitCode(cfg, results); code != 2 {
		t.Errorf("ExitCode = %d, want 2", code)
	}
}
```

```go
	results := make([]ServerResult, len(servers))
	sem := make(chan struct{}, opts.Parallel)
	var hops fleet.HopLimiter
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	for i, srv := range servers {
		if srv.JumpOnly {
			// A tunnel only (OP8): listed, never read, no slot taken.
			results[i] = ServerResult{Server: srv}
			continue
		}
		wg.Add(1)
		go func() {
			defer wg.Done()
			// The bastion slots come first and the per-server budget only
			// starts in auditOne, so a queue behind one bastion neither burns
			// a server's timeout nor holds a --parallel slot idle (spec S5).

			if err != nil {
				results[i] = canceledResult(ctx, cfg, srv)

			defer release()
			// Ctrl-C stops the queue: a server still waiting for a slot is
			// never dialed (the same select apply.Execute uses).

			case <-ctx.Done():
				results[i] = canceledResult(ctx, cfg, srv)
				return
			}

			if ctx.Err() != nil { // both select cases were ready

				return
			}

		}()
	}
	wg.Wait()
```

```go
// ServerHead is a server's report head, shared with apply's report:
// "name (user@host:port)", with " via BASTION" inside the parentheses for a
// server behind one, so a report with no via: is byte-identical.
func ServerHead(s config.Server) string {
	via := ""
	if s.Via != "" {
		via = " via " + s.Via
	}
	return fmt.Sprintf("%s (%s@%s:%d%s)", s.Name, s.User, s.Host, s.Port, via)
}
```

Replace `tallyCounts` and `tallyResults` (`:625-658`) with:

```go
// tallyCounts aggregates fleet outcomes for the summary line and the JSON
// report. A critical server counts in both drift and critical.
type tallyCounts struct {
	compliant, drift, critical, unreachable int
	hostKeyAlerts                           int // distinct host keys that changed or are revoked (alertSource)
	revokedKeys                             int // tombstoned keys installed, across reachable servers
	jumpOnly                                int // jump_only servers, listed and never read
}

func tallyResults(cfg *config.Config, results []ServerResult) tallyCounts {
	var c tallyCounts
	alerted := map[string]bool{}
	for _, r := range results {
		for _, k := range r.Diff.Unauthorized {
			if _, revoked := cfg.Revoked(k.Fingerprint); revoked && r.Err == nil {
				c.revokedKeys++
			}
		}
		switch statusOf(cfg, r) {
		case statusUnreachable:
			c.unreachable++
			if who := alertSource(r); who != "" && !alerted[who] {
				alerted[who] = true
				c.hostKeyAlerts++
			}
		case statusJumpOnly:
			c.jumpOnly++
		case statusCompliant:
			c.compliant++
		case statusCritical:
			c.drift++
			c.critical++
		default:
			c.drift++
		}
	}
	return c
}

// alertSource names whose host key alarmed for an unreachable server: its
// bastion's when the hop failed, so one changed bastion key counts once
// however many servers sit behind it, and once with the bastion's own row
// (spec S5); else the server's own. "" when nothing alarmed.
func alertSource(r ServerResult) string {
	if !hostKeyAlert(fleet.ErrorKindOf(r.Err)) {
		return ""
	}
	if hop := fleet.FailedHopOf(r.Err); hop != "" {
		return hop
	}
	return r.Server.Name
}
```

```go
package main

import (
	"bytes"
	"fmt"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"golang.org/x/crypto/ssh"

	"github.com/javadh75/SSHepherd/internal/sshconn/sshtest"
)

// bastionFleet: a jump_only bastion that forwards and a target whose file
// holds alice's key; the agent holds that key. It returns both servers,
// their host keys, the target's HOME and a manifest granting alice the
// target.
func bastionFleet(t *testing.T) (hop, target *sshtest.Server, hopKey, targetKey ssh.Signer, home, cfg string) {
	t.Helper()
	user := sshtest.Key(1)
	pub := sshtest.Signer(t, user).PublicKey()
	line := strings.TrimSpace(string(ssh.MarshalAuthorizedKey(pub)))
	home = shortHome(t)
	if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Join(home, ".ssh"), 0o700); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := os.WriteFile(filepath.Join(home, ".ssh", "authorized_keys"), []byte(line+"\n"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	hopKey, targetKey = sshtest.Signer(t, sshtest.Key(40)), sshtest.Signer(t, sshtest.Key(41))
	hop = sshtest.NewServer(t, sshtest.ServerConfig{HostKeys: []ssh.Signer{hopKey}, Authorized: []ssh.PublicKey{pub}, Forward: sshtest.DialForward})
	target = sshtest.NewServer(t, sshtest.ServerConfig{HostKeys: []ssh.Signer{targetKey}, Authorized: []ssh.PublicKey{pub}, Handle: sshtest.ShellHandler(home)})
	t.Setenv("SSH_AUTH_SOCK", sshtest.Agent(t, user))
	cfg = writeConfig(t, fmt.Sprintf(`
users:
  - {name: alice, keys: ["%s"]}
servers:
  - {name: bastion, host: 127.0.0.1, port: %d, user: jump, jump_only: true}
  - {name: web-1, host: 127.0.0.1, port: %d, user: deploy, via: bastion}
access:
  - {user: alice, servers: ["*"]}
`, line, hop.Port, target.Port))
	return hop, target, hopKey, targetKey, home, cfg
}

// TestAuditThroughBastionHermetic: audit reads the target over the
// bastion, never reads the bastion, and pages once on a changed bastion
// key with via_failed on the dependent.
func TestAuditThroughBastionHermetic(t *testing.T) {
	t.Run("compliant", func(t *testing.T) {
		hop, target, hopKey, targetKey, _, cfg := bastionFleet(t)
		kh := sshtest.KnownHostsFile(t, hop.KnownHostsLine(hopKey.PublicKey()), target.KnownHostsLine(targetKey.PublicKey()))
		var out, errBuf bytes.Buffer
		if code := run([]string{"audit", "--config", cfg, "--known-hosts", kh}, &out, &errBuf); code != 0 {
			t.Fatalf("exit = %d, want 0\nstdout: %s\nstderr: %s", code, out.String(), errBuf.String())
		}
		for _, want := range []string{
			fmt.Sprintf("bastion (jump@127.0.0.1:%d)  jump only (not audited: nothing reads its authorized_keys)", hop.Port),
			fmt.Sprintf("web-1 (deploy@127.0.0.1:%d via bastion)", target.Port),
		} {
			if !strings.Contains(out.String(), want) {
				t.Errorf("report lacks %q:\n%s", want, out.String())
			}
		}
		if !strings.Contains(errBuf.String(), "1 read, 0 unreachable, 1 jump only") {
			t.Errorf("stderr lacks the jump-only read count:\n%s", errBuf.String())
		}
		if len(hop.Commands()) != 0 || !slices.Equal(hop.Forwards(), []string{target.Addr()}) {
			t.Errorf("bastion ran %q and forwarded %v; want no command and one forward", hop.Commands(), hop.Forwards())
		}
	})
	t.Run("changed bastion key", func(t *testing.T) {
		hop, target, _, targetKey, _, cfg := bastionFleet(t)
		kh := sshtest.KnownHostsFile(t, hop.KnownHostsLine(sshtest.Signer(t, sshtest.Key(42)).PublicKey()), target.KnownHostsLine(targetKey.PublicKey()))
		var out, errBuf bytes.Buffer
		if code := run([]string{"audit", "--config", cfg, "--known-hosts", kh, "--json"}, &out, &errBuf); code != 2 {
			t.Fatalf("exit = %d, want 2\nstdout: %s", code, out.String())
		}
		for _, want := range []string{`"via_failed": "bastion"`, `"host_key_alerts": 1`, `"error_kind": "host_key_changed"`} {
			if !strings.Contains(out.String(), want) {
				t.Errorf("JSON lacks %s:\n%s", want, out.String())
			}
		}
		if len(target.Attempts()) != 0 {
			t.Error("the target was reached past a changed bastion key")
		}
	})
	t.Run("changed target key", func(t *testing.T) {
		// The bastion is fine; the server behind it presents another key:
		// the server's own alarm, exit 2, and no via_failed.
		hop, target, hopKey, _, _, cfg := bastionFleet(t)
		kh := sshtest.KnownHostsFile(t, hop.KnownHostsLine(hopKey.PublicKey()), target.KnownHostsLine(sshtest.Signer(t, sshtest.Key(43)).PublicKey()))
		var out, errBuf bytes.Buffer
		if code := run([]string{"audit", "--config", cfg, "--known-hosts", kh, "--json"}, &out, &errBuf); code != 2 {
			t.Fatalf("exit = %d, want 2\nstdout: %s", code, out.String())
		}
		if !strings.Contains(out.String(), `"host_key_alerts": 1`) || !strings.Contains(out.String(), `"error_kind": "host_key_changed"`) {
			t.Errorf("JSON lacks the target's own alert:\n%s", out.String())
		}
		if strings.Contains(out.String(), `"via_failed"`) {
			t.Errorf("a target's own changed key carries via_failed:\n%s", out.String())
		}
	})
	t.Run("a run that selects no server behind the bastion", func(t *testing.T) {
		// spec S5: such a run opens no connection to the bastion at all.
		hop, target, hopKey, targetKey, home, _ := bastionFleet(t)
		pub := sshtest.Signer(t, sshtest.Key(1)).PublicKey()
		direct := sshtest.NewServer(t, sshtest.ServerConfig{HostKeys: []ssh.Signer{targetKey}, Authorized: []ssh.PublicKey{pub}, Handle: sshtest.ShellHandler(home)})
		cfg := writeConfig(t, fmt.Sprintf(`
users:
  - {name: alice, keys: ["%s"]}
servers:
  - {name: bastion, host: 127.0.0.1, port: %d, user: jump, jump_only: true}
  - {name: web-1, host: 127.0.0.1, port: %d, user: deploy, via: bastion}
  - {name: db-1, host: 127.0.0.1, port: %d, user: deploy}
access:
  - {user: alice, servers: ["*"]}
`, strings.TrimSpace(string(ssh.MarshalAuthorizedKey(pub))), hop.Port, target.Port, direct.Port))
		kh := sshtest.KnownHostsFile(t, hop.KnownHostsLine(hopKey.PublicKey()), target.KnownHostsLine(targetKey.PublicKey()),
			direct.KnownHostsLine(targetKey.PublicKey()))
		var out, errBuf bytes.Buffer
		if code := run([]string{"audit", "--server", "db-1", "--config", cfg, "--known-hosts", kh}, &out, &errBuf); code != 0 {
			t.Fatalf("exit = %d, want 0\nstdout: %s\nstderr: %s", code, out.String(), errBuf.String())
		}
		if len(hop.Attempts()) != 0 || len(hop.Forwards()) != 0 {
			t.Errorf("bastion saw attempts %v and forwards %v; a run selecting only db-1 must not touch it", hop.Attempts(), hop.Forwards())
		}
	})
}
```

```go
// reader is phase 1's reader: the run's known_hosts snapshot and
// credentials, offering preferKeys on each dial, resolving via: through
// cfg.
func (s *fleetSession) reader(cfg *config.Config, preferKeys func(config.Server) []string) *sshread.Client {
	return &sshread.Client{KnownHostsPath: s.khPath, KnownHosts: s.kh, Creds: s.creds, DialTimeout: dialTimeout,
		PreferKeys: preferKeys, Server: cfg.ServerNamed, OfferAll: s.offerAll, QuietFallback: s.quietFallback, Log: s.log}
}

// dialer is phase 2's dialer: the same known_hosts snapshot and
// credentials, resolving via: through cfg.
func (s *fleetSession) dialer(cfg *config.Config, preferKeys func(config.Server) []string) *sshconn.Dialer {
	return &sshconn.Dialer{KnownHostsPath: s.khPath, KnownHosts: s.kh, Creds: s.creds, DialTimeout: dialTimeout,
		PreferKeys: preferKeys, Server: cfg.ServerNamed, OfferAll: s.offerAll, QuietFallback: s.quietFallback, Log: s.log}
}
```

and in `Read` (`:188`) call `s.reader(cfg, preferKeys)`. In `cmd/sshepherd/apply.go:348-349` call `pre.reader(s.cfg, s.preferKeys)` and `pre.dialer(s.cfg, s.preferKeys)`; in `cmd/sshepherd/sweep.go:61` call `sess.reader(cfg, offer)`.

Then turn TCP forwarding on for container A. Alpine's stock `/etc/ssh/sshd_config` sets `AllowTcpForwarding no` (`sshd -T` in `sshepherd-it-sshd:alpine` prints `allowtcpforwarding no`; debian and rocky print `yes`), so without this every `direct-tcpip` through A is refused on alpine, the distro both lanes gate on. Add `"AllowTcpForwarding yes"` to the existing `sshd_set` line (`:158`; `sshd_set` prepends, and sshd keeps the first value it reads), and an expectation after the other three:

```sh
    sshd_set "AuthorizedKeysFile .ssh/authorized_keys .ssh/authorized_keys2" "PermitRootLogin prohibit-password" "PerSourcePenalties no" "AllowTcpForwarding yes"
    …
    sshd_expect "allowtcpforwarding yes"
```

The `Match User jumpnofwd` block appended below still turns forwarding off for that one account (a `Match` block overrides the global value).

After the `maxauth` Match block (`:165-166`):

```sh
    printf "Match User jumpnofwd\n  AllowTcpForwarding no\n" >> /etc/ssh/sshd_config
    sshd -T -C user=jumpnofwd,host=x,addr=127.0.0.1 | grep -Eqix "allowtcpforwarding no" || { echo "sshd -T lacks AllowTcpForwarding no for jumpnofwd" >&2; exit 1; }
```

```go
// itBastion is container A's account user as a bastion, and the target on
// container C, which only A can reach.
func itBastion(t *testing.T, c *Client, host string, port int, user string, jumpOnly bool) config.Server {
	t.Helper()
	target := os.Getenv("SSHEPHERD_IT_TARGET_HOST")
	if target == "" {
		t.Fatal("SSHEPHERD_IT_TARGET_HOST not set: scripts/integration.sh must start the internal container")
	}
	bastion := config.Server{Name: "bastion-it", Host: host, Port: port, User: user, JumpOnly: jumpOnly}
	c.Server = func(name string) (config.Server, bool) { return bastion, name == bastion.Name }
	tp, err := strconv.Atoi(os.Getenv("SSHEPHERD_IT_TARGET_PORT"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("SSHEPHERD_IT_TARGET_PORT: %v", err)
	}
	return config.Server{Name: "hopped-it", Host: target, Port: tp, User: "hopped", Via: bastion.Name}
}

func TestIntegrationReadThroughBastion(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tt := range []struct {
		name, user string
		jumpOnly   bool
	}{
		{"managed bastion", "jump", false},
		{"jump_only bastion with a nologin shell", "jumpnl", true},
	} {
		t.Run(tt.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			c, host, port := itClient(t)
			srv := itBastion(t, c, host, port, tt.user, tt.jumpOnly)
			ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
			defer cancel()
			res, err := c.ReadAuthorizedKeys(ctx, srv)
			if err != nil {
				t.Fatalf("ReadAuthorizedKeys through %s: %v", tt.user, err)
			}
			if !strings.Contains(string(res.Content), "ssh-ed25519") {
				t.Errorf("content = %q, want the seeded key", res.Content)
			}
		})
	}
}

func TestIntegrationBastionRefusesForward(t *testing.T) {
	c, host, port := itClient(t)
	srv := itBastion(t, c, host, port, "jumpnofwd", true)
	ctx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), 30*time.Second)
	defer cancel()
	_, err := c.ReadAuthorizedKeys(ctx, srv)
	if fleet.ErrorKindOf(err) != fleet.ErrorKindDial || fleet.FailedHopOf(err) != "bastion-it" || fleet.HintOf(err) != sshconn.ForwardHint {
		t.Fatalf("err = %v, want a refused forward at the bastion", err)
	}
}
```

```go
// TestIntegrationWriteThroughBastion: apply's whole write protocol, the
// login probe included, through container A to container C.
func TestIntegrationWriteThroughBastion(t *testing.T) {
	d, host, port := itDialer(t)
	bastion := config.Server{Name: "bastion-it", Host: host, Port: port, User: "jump", JumpOnly: true}
	d.Server = func(name string) (config.Server, bool) { return bastion, name == bastion.Name }
	tp, err := strconv.Atoi(os.Getenv("SSHEPHERD_IT_TARGET_PORT"))
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("SSHEPHERD_IT_TARGET_PORT: %v", err)
	}
	srv := config.Server{Name: "hopw-it", Host: os.Getenv("SSHEPHERD_IT_TARGET_HOST"), Port: tp, User: "hopw", Via: bastion.Name}
	reseed(t, d, srv, fixture{keys: strp(itDrifted(t))})
	w := &FleetWriter{Dialer: d}
	if err := w.Apply(itCtx(t), srv, itRequest(t, d, srv, itRendered(t))); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Apply through the bastion: %v", err)
	}
	if got := remoteOut(t, d, srv, `cat "$HOME/.ssh/authorized_keys"`); got != string(itRendered(t)) {
		t.Errorf("file = %q, want the render", got)
	}
}

// TestIntegrationForwardProbeOnBastion: writing a bastion probes a forward
// to a dependent: allowed keeps the write, AllowTcpForwarding no restores.
func TestIntegrationForwardProbeOnBastion(t *testing.T) {
	forward := net.JoinHostPort(os.Getenv("SSHEPHERD_IT_TARGET_HOST"), os.Getenv("SSHEPHERD_IT_TARGET_PORT"))
	for _, tt := range []struct {
		user     string
		restored bool
	}{
		{"jumpw", false},
		{"jumpnofwd", true},
	} {
		t.Run(tt.user, func(t *testing.T) {
			d, host, port := itDialer(t)
			srv := config.Server{Name: tt.user + "-it", Host: host, Port: port, User: tt.user}
			reseed(t, d, srv, fixture{keys: strp(itDrifted(t))})
			req := itRequest(t, d, srv, itRendered(t))
			req.ProbeForward = forward
			err := (&FleetWriter{Dialer: d}).Apply(itCtx(t), srv, req)
			var lockout *fleet.LockoutError
			switch {
			case tt.restored && (!errors.As(err, &lockout) || lockout.Restore != nil):
				t.Fatalf("Apply = %v, want a restored lockout", err)
			case !tt.restored && err != nil:
				t.Fatalf("Apply = %v, want success", err)
			}
			want := string(itRendered(t))
			if tt.restored {
				want = itDrifted(t)
			}
			if got := remoteOut(t, d, srv, `cat "$HOME/.ssh/authorized_keys"`); got != want {
				t.Errorf("file = %q, want %q", got, want)
			}
		})
	}
}
```

**docsDelta (README, lane `transport`).** The integrator copies every sample from the goldens named, never from this text.

1. **Requirements** (`README.md:149-151`): replace the "Direct reachability" bullet with:

```markdown
- **Reachability.** Each server is dialed directly, or through the bastions its `via:`
  names (at most 3 deep, see [Bastions](#bastions-via)). Each hop is its own SSH
  connection with strict `known_hosts`, so every bastion and every server behind one
  needs its own recorded key. A private host's key must be recorded under the address
  the manifest uses (`10.0.1.5`, not the bastion's view of a hostname), verified out of
  band: on its console, or with the scan the `host_key_unknown` hint prints, which runs
  on the bastion (`ssh -p 22 jump@bastion.example.com ssh-keyscan … | ssh-keygen -lf -`).
```

2. **The manifest**: add a `### Bastions (via:)` subsection after "Per-server `authorized_keys_file`", holding: the design's YAML example (`bastion-eu` with `jump_only: true`, `web-1` with `via: bastion-eu`); "**A bastion without `jump_only` is a normal managed server:** apply rewrites its whole file like any other server's, a `servers: ["*"]` entry or an operator's implicit grant puts those users on it, a bastion no grant reaches is refused (exit 1) while its file holds keys, and its account becomes part of every dependent's reach."; "**`jump_only: true`** makes it a tunnel and nothing else: `*`, `@tag` and the operator's implicit grant skip it, `access:` cannot name it, `audit`/`apply` list it as `jump only` without reading or writing it, `adopt` and `--sweep` do not read it, and its host key is still checked strictly whenever a run tunnels through it. Nothing reads its `authorized_keys`, so a leaver's key there survives `revoke`; the flag on a real server hides it from audit. `jump_only_unused` warns when no server names it."; "**Key offering:** a managed bastion is offered the keys granted on it (falling back to every loaded key with a warning); a `jump_only` bastion every loaded key, with no warning."; "**One address, one entry, one route.** `known_hosts` is keyed by address, so two entries for one target, or two entries at one `host:port` behind different hops, are refused. **Known limitation:** two different machines that share a private address behind different bastions (say `10.0.0.5` in two networks) cannot both be in one manifest by address. Give each a hostname its own bastion resolves; a future `host_key_alias:` field (OpenSSH's `HostKeyAlias`) would lift this."; "**Concurrency:** at most 8 connections through one bastion at a time (a fixed cap, below sshd's default `MaxStartups`); a write holds two (its session and its login check). A three-hop chain can use most of the 30-second per-server timeout."

A bastion that refuses the forward (`AllowTcpForwarding no`, or a `no-port-forwarding`/`restrict`/`permitopen=` line) gives the hint `the bastion refused the forward; allow TCP forwarding for this account`. A bastion that opens the channel but cannot reach the server is the server's own failure (no `via_failed`), with the hint `the bastion could not reach this server; check that it is up and reachable from the bastion`." **docsDelta (CLAUDE.md, lane `transport`).** Append to the S5 paragraph:

```markdown
`sshconn.ConnectWith` stays the one choke point: `dialTarget` reaches a via server over a
`direct-tcpip` channel through a fresh connection to its bastion (`Dialer.Server` =
`cfg.ServerNamed`; no lookup fails closed), one hop per target, never pooled; `Conn` keeps its hop
and raw transport, `Close` always closes the hop, cancellation closes tunnel and hop, and
`setDeadline` moves the hop's socket with `SessionTimeout`. A hop failure is
`sshconn.Error.HopStage` (`fleet.FailedHopOf`, JSON `via_failed`); a `Prohibited` forward is kind
`dial` with `ForwardHint`; a target the bastion cannot reach is the target's own `dial` with
`HopDialHint`. A
`jump_only` hop is offered every held key with no warning. The via host-key hint scans on the bastion.
`fleet.HopLimiter` (`HopCap` 8) is taken in `audit.Run` before the per-server budget; a
`jump_only` row is never read; `host_key_alerts` counts one per failing bastion. The login probe
treats a hop failure as "could not check" (UNPROVEN, never a restore) and, on a bastion, opens
`fleet.WriteRequest.ProbeForward` (a `Prohibited` forward restores). `scripts/integration.sh` adds an
`--internal` network, container C (`hopped`, `hopw`, `hope2e`) and bastion accounts `jump`, `jumpnl`
(nologin), `jumpw`, `jumpnofwd` (AllowTcpForwarding no) on A, which sets `AllowTcpForwarding yes`
globally (alpine's stock config says no).
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement.** Create `internal/sshcfg/jump.go`:

```go
package sshcfg

import (
	"net"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
)

// JumpHop is one hop of a ProxyJump value, [user@]host[:port]. User is ""
// and Port 0 when the hop names none.
type JumpHop struct {
	User string
	Host string
	Port int
}

// String renders the hop as ProxyJump spells it.
func (h JumpHop) String() string {
	s := h.Host
	if h.Port != 0 {
		s = net.JoinHostPort(h.Host, strconv.Itoa(h.Port))
	}
	if h.User != "" {
		s = h.User + "@" + s
	}
	return s
}

// ParseJump splits a Host.Proxy of "ProxyJump a,b" into its hops in the
// order ssh connects to them, a first. ok is false for a ProxyCommand, an
// empty hop, an ssh:// URI, a bad port or a bracketed address without one,
// or whitespace: import leaves those unmapped and warns (spec S5 Import).
func ParseJump(proxy string) ([]JumpHop, bool) {
	spec, isJump := strings.CutPrefix(proxy, "ProxyJump ")
	if !isJump || spec == "" || strings.ContainsAny(spec, " \t") {
		return nil, false
	}
	var hops []JumpHop
	for _, part := range strings.Split(spec, ",") {
		h, ok := parseHop(part)
		if !ok {
			return nil, false
		}
		hops = append(hops, h)
	}
	return hops, true
}

// parseHop reads one [user@]host[:port]; the user ends at the last '@', as
// ssh reads it.
func parseHop(s string) (JumpHop, bool) {
	if s == "" || strings.Contains(s, "://") {
		return JumpHop{}, false
	}
	var h JumpHop
	if at := strings.LastIndex(s, "@"); at >= 0 {
		h.User, s = s[:at], s[at+1:]
		if h.User == "" {
			return JumpHop{}, false
		}
	}
	h.Host = s
	if host, port, err := net.SplitHostPort(s); err == nil {
		p, perr := strconv.Atoi(port)
		if perr != nil || p < 1 || p > 65535 {
			return JumpHop{}, false
		}
		h.Host, h.Port = host, p
	} else if strings.HasPrefix(s, "[") || strings.Count(s, ":") == 1 {
		return JumpHop{}, false // "[v6]" with no port, "host:", "host:x"
	}
	if h.Host == "" || strings.ContainsAny(h.Host, "/[]") {
		return JumpHop{}, false
	}
	return h, true
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: The fuzz target and its corpus.** Append to `internal/sshcfg/fuzz_test.go` (add `"slices"` and `"strings"` to its imports):

```go
// FuzzParseJump asserts ParseJump never panics, accepts only well-formed
// hops, and that what it accepts renders back to itself: the hops import
// maps are exactly the ones the config spelled.
func FuzzParseJump(f *testing.F) {
	for _, s := range []string{
		"ProxyJump bastion",
		"ProxyJump jump@bastion.example.com:2200,inner",
		"ProxyJump [2001:db8::1]:22",
		"ProxyCommand nc %h %p",
		"ProxyJump ssh://u@h:22",
		"ProxyJump a,,b",
	} {
		f.Add(s)
	}
	f.Fuzz(func(t *testing.T, in string) {
		hops, ok := ParseJump(in)
		if !ok {
			return
		}
		parts := make([]string, 0, len(hops))
		for _, h := range hops {
			if h.Host == "" || h.Port < 0 || h.Port > 65535 || strings.ContainsAny(h.Host+h.User, ", \t") {
				t.Fatalf("ParseJump(%q) accepted hop %+v", in, h)
			}
			parts = append(parts, h.String())
		}
		again, ok := ParseJump("ProxyJump " + strings.Join(parts, ","))
		if !ok || !slices.Equal(again, hops) {
			t.Fatalf("ParseJump(%q) = %+v, but its rendering parses to %+v (ok %v)", in, hops, again, ok)
		}
	})
}
```

```go
func jh(alias, hostname, user, proxy string) sshcfg.Host {
	return sshcfg.Host{Alias: alias, HostName: hostname, User: user, Proxy: proxy}

// TestMapRoutes is the import mapping table (spec S5 Import, gap item 11).
func TestMapRoutes(t *testing.T) {
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement.** Create `internal/reconcile/routes.go`:

```go
package reconcile

import (
	"fmt"
	"slices"
	"strings"

	"github.com/javadh75/SSHepherd/internal/config"
	"github.com/javadh75/SSHepherd/internal/sshcfg"
)

// Routes is an ssh config's ProxyJump chains mapped onto server entries
// (spec S5 Import): the via each host gets, how each host is reached, and
// why a host's proxy could not be mapped. The zero value maps nothing.
type Routes struct {
	via map[string]string // alias -> its via, for every imported host on a mapped chain
	key map[string]string // alias -> its route: "" direct, the resolved chain, or "?" + the raw proxy
	why map[string]string // alias -> why its ProxyJump/ProxyCommand is not mapped
}

// Via is the via: a host gets; "" when it is direct or not mapped.
func (r Routes) Via(alias string) string { return r.via[alias] }

// NotMapped reports why alias's proxy is not mapped.
func (r Routes) NotMapped(alias string) (string, bool) {
	why, ok := r.why[alias]
	return why, ok
}

// routeKey is how alias reaches its address: "" direct, its bastions root
// first and comma-joined, or "?" and the raw proxy when that is not mapped.
// Two hosts at one address are one machine only when their keys agree.
func (r Routes) routeKey(alias string) string { return r.key[alias] }

// hopNode is a server a ProxyJump hop can name: an imported host, or a
// manifest server (fixed: the manifest's via wins).
type hopNode struct {
	name, host, user string
	port             int
	fixed            bool
	via              string // the manifest's via (fixed only)
}

// hopIndex is every server a hop can resolve to, manifest servers first.
type hopIndex struct {
	nodes   map[string]hopNode
	order   []string
	proxyOf map[string]string // alias -> its raw Proxy
	// differs holds every alias that names a manifest server and, in the ssh
	// config, a host with another host, port or user (the builder keeps the
	// manifest's and marks the incoming one conflicted). A hop by that alias
	// cannot tell which machine the operator meant, so it is not mapped.
	differs map[string]bool
}

func newHopIndex(hosts []sshcfg.Host, manifest []config.Server) hopIndex {
	x := hopIndex{nodes: map[string]hopNode{}, proxyOf: map[string]string{}, differs: map[string]bool{}}
	for _, s := range manifest {
		x.add(hopNode{name: s.Name, host: s.Host, user: s.User, port: s.Port, fixed: true, via: s.Via})
	}
	for _, h := range hosts {
		x.proxyOf[h.Alias] = h.Proxy
		n, taken := x.nodes[h.Alias]
		switch {
		case h.User == "":
		case !taken:
			x.add(hopNode{name: h.Alias, host: h.HostName, user: h.User, port: h.Port})
		case n.fixed && !sameMachine(n, h):
			x.differs[h.Alias] = true
		}
	}
	return x
}

// sameMachine is the builder's "identical" test (builder.servers): host,
// user and effective port all equal.
func sameMachine(n hopNode, h sshcfg.Host) bool {
	port := h.Port
	if port == 0 {
		port = 22
	}
	return n.host == h.HostName && n.user == h.User && n.port == port
}

func (x *hopIndex) add(n hopNode) {
	if n.port == 0 {
		n.port = 22
	}
	x.nodes[n.name] = n
	x.order = append(x.order, n.name)
}

// MapRoutes maps every server-worthy host's ProxyJump chain onto servers. A
// chain maps when each hop resolves to exactly one imported host or
// manifest server, no server it constrains would need two different
// parents (another chain, its own route, or the manifest's via), and the
// whole route — the first hop's own route included, since ssh connects a
// hop with that hop's own config — neither loops, nor passes a host whose
// proxy is not mapped, nor runs deeper than config.MaxViaDepth. manifest is
// nil for a fresh import; a host the manifest already names keeps the
// manifest's route.
func MapRoutes(hosts []sshcfg.Host, manifest []config.Server) Routes {
	x := newHopIndex(hosts, manifest)
	r := Routes{via: map[string]string{}, key: map[string]string{}, why: map[string]string{}}
	chains := map[string][]string{}
	for _, h := range hosts {
		if h.User == "" || h.Proxy == "" || x.nodes[h.Alias].fixed {
			continue
		}
		if names, why := x.resolveChain(h); why != "" {
			r.why[h.Alias] = why
		} else {
			chains[h.Alias] = names
		}
	}
	need := x.parentNeeds(hosts, chains, r.why)
	for _, alias := range x.order {
		names, ok := chains[alias]
		if !ok {
			continue
		}
		if clash := firstClash(append(slices.Clone(names[1:]), alias), need); clash != "" {
			r.why[alias] = fmt.Sprintf("hop %q would need two different parents", clash)
			continue
		}
		r.via[alias] = names[len(names)-1]
		for i := 1; i < len(names); i++ {
			if !x.nodes[names[i]].fixed {
				r.via[names[i]] = names[i-1]
			}
		}
	}
	x.dropBroken(&r)
	for _, alias := range x.order {
		if _, bad := r.why[alias]; bad {
			r.key[alias] = "?" + x.proxyOf[alias]
			continue
		}
		chain, _ := x.walk(r, alias)
		r.key[alias] = strings.Join(chain, ",")
	}
	return r
}

// resolveChain resolves h's ProxyJump hops to server names, first hop
// first, or says why it cannot.
func (x hopIndex) resolveChain(h sshcfg.Host) ([]string, string) {
	hops, ok := sshcfg.ParseJump(h.Proxy)
	switch {
	case !ok:
		return nil, "only ProxyJump [user@]host[:port] hops are mapped"
	case len(hops) > config.MaxViaDepth:
		return nil, fmt.Sprintf("%d hops; at most %d", len(hops), config.MaxViaDepth)
	}
	names := make([]string, 0, len(hops))
	for _, hop := range hops {
		name, why := x.resolve(hop)
		if why != "" {
			return nil, why
		}
		if name == h.Alias || slices.Contains(names, name) {
			return nil, fmt.Sprintf("the chain loops back to %q", name)
		}
		names = append(names, name)
	}
	return names, ""
}

// resolve finds the one server a hop names: the alias it spells, else the
// servers at its host and port (22 when unset) whose user matches (any
// when unset). An explicit user or port must agree either way.
func (x hopIndex) resolve(hop sshcfg.JumpHop) (string, string) {
	if x.differs[hop.Host] {
		return "", fmt.Sprintf("hop %q names manifest server %q, but the ssh config's host of that name is another machine (host, port or user differ)", hop.String(), hop.Host)
	}
	if n, ok := x.nodes[hop.Host]; ok {
		if (hop.User != "" && hop.User != n.user) || (hop.Port != 0 && hop.Port != n.port) {
			return "", fmt.Sprintf("hop %q names server %q with another user or port", hop.String(), n.name)
		}
		return n.name, ""
	}
	port := hop.Port
	if port == 0 {
		port = 22
	}
	var found []string
	for _, name := range x.order {
		n := x.nodes[name]
		if strings.EqualFold(n.host, hop.Host) && n.port == port && (hop.User == "" || hop.User == n.user) {
			found = append(found, name)
		}
	}
	switch len(found) {
	case 1:
		return found[0], ""
	case 0:
		return "", fmt.Sprintf("hop %q is not a server in the ssh config or the manifest", hop.String())
	}
	return "", fmt.Sprintf("hop %q matches %d servers; name one by its alias", hop.String(), len(found))
}

// parentNeeds collects every parent asked of each server: a manifest
// server's via, an imported host's own route ("" direct, "?" an unmapped
// proxy, else its chain's last hop), and each chain's demand on every hop
// after its first.
func (x hopIndex) parentNeeds(hosts []sshcfg.Host, chains map[string][]string, why map[string]string) map[string]map[string]bool {
	need := map[string]map[string]bool{}
	add := func(name, parent string) {
		if need[name] == nil {
			need[name] = map[string]bool{}
		}
		need[name][parent] = true
	}
	for _, name := range x.order {
		if n := x.nodes[name]; n.fixed {
			add(name, n.via)
		}
	}
	for _, h := range hosts {
		switch {
		case h.User == "" || x.nodes[h.Alias].fixed:
		case h.Proxy == "":
			add(h.Alias, "")
		case why[h.Alias] != "":
			add(h.Alias, "?")
		default:
			names := chains[h.Alias]
			add(h.Alias, names[len(names)-1])
		}
	}
	for _, names := range chains {
		for i := 1; i < len(names); i++ {
			add(names[i], names[i-1])
		}
	}
	return need
}

// firstClash names the first server asked for two different parents.
func firstClash(names []string, need map[string]map[string]bool) string {
	for _, n := range names {
		if len(need[n]) > 1 {
			return n
		}
	}
	return ""
}

// viaOf is a server's via: the manifest's for a manifest server, the
// mapped one for an imported host.
func (x hopIndex) viaOf(r Routes, name string) string {
	if n := x.nodes[name]; n.fixed {
		return n.via
	}
	return r.via[name]
}

// walk lists name's bastions root first, or says why the route is
// unusable: it loops, passes a host whose own proxy is not mapped, or runs
// deeper than config.MaxViaDepth.
func (x hopIndex) walk(r Routes, name string) ([]string, string) {
	var chain []string
	seen := map[string]bool{name: true}
	for v := x.viaOf(r, name); v != ""; v = x.viaOf(r, v) {
		switch {
		case seen[v]:
			return nil, fmt.Sprintf("the chain loops back to %q", v)
		case r.why[v] != "":
			return nil, fmt.Sprintf("hop %q has a proxy that is not mapped", v)
		}
		seen[v] = true
		chain = append(chain, v)
	}
	if len(chain) > config.MaxViaDepth {
		return nil, fmt.Sprintf("the route is %d hops; at most %d", len(chain), config.MaxViaDepth)
	}
	slices.Reverse(chain)
	return chain, ""
}

// dropBroken unmaps every host whose whole route walk refuses. Every
// verdict is taken against the routes as mapped, before any deletion, so
// each host is told its own reason (every host of a loop reads "loops
// back", not "has a proxy that is not mapped" because a sibling was dropped
// first). One pass is a fixed point: a route through a dropped host
// contains that host's whole route, so its own walk refuses in the same
// pass, for the same reason.
func (x hopIndex) dropBroken(r *Routes) {
	broken := map[string]string{}
	for _, name := range x.order {
		if r.via[name] == "" {
			continue
		}
		if _, why := x.walk(*r, name); why != "" {
			broken[name] = why
		}
	}
	for name, why := range broken {
		delete(r.via, name)
		r.why[name] = why
	}
}
```

and add:

```go
// newServer appends one incoming host the manifest does not name, unless
// its target or its address is taken: the same route makes it the same
// machine, so its grants move there; another route may be another
// machine (OP7), so it is a conflict and its grants are dropped.
func (b *builder) newServer(h sshcfg.Host, targets map[target]string, at map[string]string) {
	t := targetOf(h.HostName, h.Port, h.User, "")
	route := b.routes.routeKey(h.Alias)
	if owner, dup := targets[t]; dup {
		b.plan.conflicts++
		if route == b.routeOf(owner) {
			b.aliasOf[h.Alias] = owner
			b.plan.Warnings = append(b.plan.Warnings, Warning{Kind: KindMergeConflict, Server: h.Alias,
				Message: sameTarget(h.Alias, owner, "manifest server")})
			return
		}
		b.conflicted[h.Alias] = true
		b.plan.Warnings = append(b.plan.Warnings, Warning{Kind: KindMergeConflict, Server: h.Alias,
			Message: otherRoute(h.Alias, "manifest server "+strconv.Quote(owner), owner)})
		return
	}
	if owner, there := at[t.address()]; there && route != b.routeOf(owner) {
		b.plan.conflicts++
		b.conflicted[h.Alias] = true
		b.plan.Warnings = append(b.plan.Warnings, Warning{Kind: KindMergeConflict, Server: h.Alias,
			Message: otherAddressRoute(h.Alias, "manifest server "+strconv.Quote(owner))})
		return
	}
	targets[t] = h.Alias
	if _, there := at[t.address()]; !there {
		at[t.address()] = h.Alias
	}
	port := h.Port
	if port == 22 {
		port = 0 // effective default, omitted on render
	}
	b.plan.NewServers = append(b.plan.NewServers,
		ServerAdd{Name: h.Alias, Host: h.HostName, Port: port, User: h.User, Via: b.routes.Via(h.Alias)})
	b.fleet[h.Alias] = true
}

// routeOf is a server's route key: the manifest's chain for a manifest
// server, the mapped route for one appended in this run.
func (b *builder) routeOf(name string) string {
	if s, ok := b.cfg.ServerNamed(name); ok {
		return strings.Join(b.cfg.Chain(s), ",")
	}
	return b.routes.routeKey(name)
}

// dropDanglingVia fixes a new server's via once every host is decided: a
// via onto a host that collapsed into a manifest server moves there (one
// machine); a via onto a host that was not appended is cleared and warned,
// so the output still loads.
func (b *builder) dropDanglingVia() {
	for i := range b.plan.NewServers {
		s := &b.plan.NewServers[i]
		if to, ok := b.aliasOf[s.Via]; ok {
			s.Via = to
		}
		if s.Via == "" || b.known(s.Via) {
			continue
		}
		b.plan.Warnings = append(b.plan.Warnings, Warning{Kind: KindProxyNotMapped, Server: s.Name, Message: fmt.Sprintf(
			"host %q: its bastion %q was not added (it conflicts with the manifest) — dialed directly; add via: by hand", s.Name, s.Via)})
		s.Via = ""
	}
}

// known reports a manifest server or one appended in this run.
func (b *builder) known(name string) bool {
	if _, ok := b.cfg.ServerNamed(name); ok {
		return true
	}
	return slices.ContainsFunc(b.plan.NewServers, func(s ServerAdd) bool { return s.Name == name })
}
```

5. After `sameTarget` (`:476-481`) add:

```go
// otherRoute words the skip of a host whose target another host owns but
// whose route differs: possibly another machine, so its grants stay put.
// who names the owner ("web-a", or manifest server "web-a").
func otherRoute(alias, who, owner string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("server %q in the ssh config has the same host, port and user as %s but a different route — skipped, and its access is not moved to %q; a manifest holds one entry per address", alias, who, owner)
}

// otherAddressRoute words the skip of a host at a taken address through
// another route (OP7: every entry at one address uses one route).
func otherAddressRoute(alias, who string) string {
	return fmt.Sprintf("server %q in the ssh config is at the same address as %s but takes a different route — skipped, and its access is not moved; every entry at one address must use the same route", alias, who)
}

// address is the target's host and port: known_hosts' key.
func (t target) address() string { return net.JoinHostPort(t.host, strconv.Itoa(t.port)) }
```

6. Replace `CollapseTargets` (`:503-539`) with:

```go
// CollapseTargets enforces one server entry per remote file among freshly
// converted hosts, for both import modes: config validation rejects two
// entries with the same (host, port, user, authorized_keys file), and
// `Host web1 web1.example.com` routinely yields two aliases for one machine.
// The first host (input order) keeps the target. A later one with the same
// route is the same machine: dropped with a warning naming both aliases,
// its users' grants redirected onto the kept alias. A later one with
// another route (MapRoutes), or at the kept host's address over another
// route even for another user, may be another machine (OP7): dropped, its
// grants removed, not moved. Hosts with no User pass through untouched.
// Inputs are not modified; users is nil under --servers-only.
func CollapseTargets(hosts []sshcfg.Host, users []identity.User) ([]sshcfg.Host, []identity.User, []string) {
	var warnings []string
	routes := MapRoutes(hosts, nil)
	owner := make(map[target]string, len(hosts))
	at := make(map[string]string, len(hosts)) // address -> first alias there
	aliasOf := make(map[string]string)
	dropped := make(map[string]bool)
	kept := make([]sshcfg.Host, 0, len(hosts))
	for _, h := range hosts {
		if h.User == "" {
			kept = append(kept, h)
			continue
		}
		t := targetOf(h.HostName, h.Port, h.User, "")
		route := routes.routeKey(h.Alias)
		if first, dup := owner[t]; dup {
			if route == routes.routeKey(first) {
				aliasOf[h.Alias] = first
				warnings = append(warnings, sameTarget(h.Alias, first, "host"))
			} else {
				dropped[h.Alias] = true
				warnings = append(warnings, otherRoute(h.Alias, strconv.Quote(first), first))
			}
			continue
		}
		if first, there := at[t.address()]; there && route != routes.routeKey(first) {
			dropped[h.Alias] = true
			warnings = append(warnings, otherAddressRoute(h.Alias, strconv.Quote(first)))
			continue
		}
		owner[t] = h.Alias
		if _, there := at[t.address()]; !there {
			at[t.address()] = h.Alias
		}
		kept = append(kept, h)
	}
	if len(aliasOf) == 0 && len(dropped) == 0 {
		return kept, users, warnings
	}
	out := make([]identity.User, len(users))
	for i, u := range users {
		u.Servers = slices.DeleteFunc(slices.Clone(remap(u.Servers, aliasOf)), func(s string) bool { return dropped[s] })
		out[i] = u
	}
	return kept, out, warnings
}
```

```go
// jumpOnly reports a manifest server marked jump_only: no grant may name it
// (spec S5, OP8).
func (b *builder) jumpOnly(srv string) bool {
	s, ok := b.cfg.ServerNamed(srv)
	return ok && s.JumpOnly
}

// noteJumpOnly records, once per server, that a key used on a jump_only
// server added no access there.
func (b *builder) noteJumpOnly(srv string) {
	if b.jumpNoted[srv] {
		return
	}
	b.jumpNoted[srv] = true
	b.plan.Notes = append(b.plan.Notes, fmt.Sprintf("server %q is jump_only in the manifest; no access added", srv))
}
```

```go
// TestImportWarnsUnmappedProxies: a proxy import cannot map is flagged with
// why; a mapped ProxyJump and a direct host are not (spec S5 Import).
func TestImportWarnsUnmappedProxies(t *testing.T) {
	hermeticHome(t)
	path := writeSSHConfig(t, "Host bastion\n  HostName bastion.example.com\n  User jump\n"+
		"Host inner\n  HostName 10.1.0.5\n  User u\n  ProxyJump bastion\n"+
		"Host lost\n  HostName 10.1.0.6\n  User u\n  ProxyJump gw\n"+
		"Host cmd\n  User u\n  ProxyCommand ssh -W %h:%p gw\nHost direct\n  User u\n")
	var out, errBuf bytes.Buffer
	if code := run([]string{"import", path, "--servers-only"}, &out, &errBuf); code != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("exit = %d, want 0 (stderr: %s)", code, errBuf.String())
	}
	for _, want := range []string{
		`host "lost": ProxyJump gw not mapped to via: (hop "gw" is not a server in the ssh config or the manifest) — dialed directly; add via: by hand`,
		`host "cmd": ProxyCommand ssh -W %h:%p gw not mapped to via: (only ProxyJump [user@]host[:port] hops are mapped) — dialed directly; add via: by hand`,
	} {
		if !strings.Contains(errBuf.String(), want) {
			t.Errorf("stderr = %q, want %q", errBuf.String(), want)
		}
	}
	for _, quiet := range []string{`"inner"`, `"direct"`, `"bastion"`} {
		if strings.Contains(errBuf.String(), quiet) {
			t.Errorf("stderr = %q, %s must not be flagged", errBuf.String(), quiet)
		}
	}
	if !strings.Contains(out.String(), "via: bastion") {
		t.Errorf("manifest lacks inner's via:\n%s", out.String())
	}
}
```

```go
package main

import (
	"bytes"
	"os"
	"path/filepath"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/javadh75/SSHepherd/internal/config"
	"github.com/javadh75/SSHepherd/internal/testkeys"
)

// TestImportMapsProxyJumpGolden: a two-hop ProxyJump chain becomes via:
// on the target and on the inner hop; the output loads.
func TestImportMapsProxyJumpGolden(t *testing.T) {
	hermeticHome(t)
	path := writeSSHConfig(t, "Host bastion\n  HostName bastion.example.com\n  User jump\n"+
		"Host inner\n  HostName 10.0.0.9\n  User ops\n  ProxyJump bastion\n"+
		"Host web\n  HostName 10.0.1.5\n  User deploy\n  ProxyJump bastion,inner\n")
	var out, errBuf bytes.Buffer
	if code := run([]string{"import", path, "--servers-only"}, &out, &errBuf); code != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("exit = %d (stderr: %s)", code, errBuf.String())
	}
	got := strings.ReplaceAll(out.String(), path, "SSHCONFIG")
	checkGolden(t, "import_manifest_via.golden", []byte(got))
	cfg, err := config.Parse(out.Bytes())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("output does not load: %v", err)
	}
	web, _ := cfg.ServerNamed("web")
	if chain := cfg.Chain(web); strings.Join(chain, ",") != "bastion,inner" {
		t.Errorf("Chain(web) = %v, want [bastion inner]", chain)
	}
}

// TestImportDropsSameIPBehindAnotherRoute: the same IP direct and behind a
// bastion keeps one entry, moves no grant, and the output loads (OP7).
func TestImportDropsSameIPBehindAnotherRoute(t *testing.T) {
	home := hermeticHome(t)
	keyPath := filepath.Join(home, "webb") // not a default identity: only web-b uses it
	if err := os.WriteFile(keyPath+".pub", []byte(testkeys.Line(t, 7)+"\n"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	path := writeSSHConfig(t, "Host bastion\n  HostName bastion.example.com\n  User jump\n"+
		"Host web-a\n  HostName 10.0.0.5\n  User deploy\n"+
		"Host web-b\n  HostName 10.0.0.5\n  User deploy\n  ProxyJump bastion\n  IdentityFile "+keyPath+"\n")
	var out, errBuf bytes.Buffer
	if code := run([]string{"import", path}, &out, &errBuf); code != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("exit = %d (stderr: %s)", code, errBuf.String())
	}
	if !strings.Contains(errBuf.String(), `server "web-b" in the ssh config has the same host, port and user as "web-a" but a different route — skipped, and its access is not moved to "web-a"`) {
		t.Errorf("stderr lacks the route warning: %s", errBuf.String())
	}
	cfg, err := config.Parse(out.Bytes())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("output does not load: %v\n%s", err, out.String())
	}
	if len(cfg.Access) != 0 {
		t.Errorf("access = %+v, want none: web-b's grant must not move to web-a", cfg.Access)
	}
}

// TestImportHopOnDroppedHostNotMapped: a ProxyJump naming a host that was
// dropped as another route is not mapped (proxy_not_mapped), and the
// dependent stays direct (spec S5 Import).
func TestImportHopOnDroppedHostNotMapped(t *testing.T) {
	hermeticHome(t)
	path := writeSSHConfig(t, "Host bastion\n  HostName bastion.example.com\n  User jump\n"+
		"Host web-a\n  HostName 10.0.0.5\n  User deploy\n"+
		"Host web-b\n  HostName 10.0.0.5\n  User deploy\n  ProxyJump bastion\n"+
		"Host app\n  HostName 10.0.1.7\n  User deploy\n  ProxyJump web-b\n")
	var out, errBuf bytes.Buffer
	if code := run([]string{"import", path, "--servers-only"}, &out, &errBuf); code != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("exit = %d (stderr: %s)", code, errBuf.String())
	}
	if want := `host "app": ProxyJump web-b not mapped to via: (hop "web-b" is not a server in the ssh config or the manifest)`; !strings.Contains(errBuf.String(), want) {
		t.Errorf("stderr = %q, want %q", errBuf.String(), want)
	}
	cfg, err := config.Parse(out.Bytes())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("output does not load: %v\n%s", err, out.String())
	}
	if app, ok := cfg.ServerNamed("app"); !ok || app.Via != "" {
		t.Errorf("app = %+v (found %v), want it present with no via", app, ok)
	}
}

// TestImportMergeRouteConflict (spec S5 Import, --merge): an incoming host
// at a manifest server's target by another route is a conflict whose
// grants are not moved; a user left with no servers gets no access entry;
// a new server behind a manifest bastion gets its via; the output loads.
func TestImportMergeRouteConflict(t *testing.T) {
	home := hermeticHome(t)
	keyPath := filepath.Join(home, "webb") // not a default identity: only web-b uses it
	if err := os.WriteFile(keyPath+".pub", []byte(testkeys.Line(t, 7)+"\n"), 0o600); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	m := writeConfig(t, "users:\n  - {name: alice, keys: [\""+testkeys.Line(t, 1)+"\"]}\n"+
		"servers:\n  - {name: web-a, host: 10.0.0.5, user: deploy}\n  - {name: bastion-eu, host: bastion.eu, user: jump, jump_only: true}\n"+
		"access:\n  - {user: alice, servers: [web-a]}\n")
	path := writeSSHConfig(t, "Host web-b\n  HostName 10.0.0.5\n  User deploy\n  ProxyJump bastion-eu\n  IdentityFile "+keyPath+"\n"+
		"Host app\n  HostName 10.0.1.7\n  User deploy\n  ProxyJump bastion-eu\n")
	var out, errBuf bytes.Buffer
	if code := run([]string{"import", path, "--merge", m}, &out, &errBuf); code != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("exit = %d (stderr: %s)", code, errBuf.String())
	}
	if want := `server "web-b" in the ssh config has the same host, port and user as manifest server "web-a" but a different route — skipped, and its access is not moved to "web-a"`; !strings.Contains(errBuf.String(), want) {
		t.Errorf("stderr lacks %q:\n%s", want, errBuf.String())
	}
	cfg, err := config.Parse(out.Bytes())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("output does not load: %v\n%s", err, out.String())
	}
	if len(cfg.Access) != 1 || cfg.Access[0].User != "alice" {
		t.Errorf("access = %+v, want alice's entry only: web-b's user has no server left", cfg.Access)
	}
	if app, ok := cfg.ServerNamed("app"); !ok || app.Via != "bastion-eu" {
		t.Errorf("app = %+v (found %v), want via: bastion-eu (renderMergedImport carries Via)", app, ok)
	}
	if strings.Contains(errBuf.String(), "not mapped to via") {
		t.Errorf("a hop naming a manifest server was flagged:\n%s", errBuf.String())
	}
}
```

```go
// TestImportJSONCarriesVia: new_servers[].via names a mapped ProxyJump's
// bastion (additive, schema 1); a direct server has no via key at all.
func TestImportJSONCarriesVia(t *testing.T) {

		"Host web\n  HostName 10.0.1.5\n  User deploy\n  ProxyJump bastion\n")

		NewServers []map[string]any `json:"new_servers"`
	}

		t.Fatalf("stdout is not a document: %v\n%s", err, out.String())

	vias := map[string]any{}
	for _, s := range doc.NewServers {

			vias[fmt.Sprint(s["name"])] = v
		}
	}
	if len(vias) != 1 || vias["web"] != "bastion" {
		t.Errorf("new_servers vias = %v, want only web via bastion", vias)
	}
}
```

2. Replace `warnProxies` (`:205-213`) with:

```go
// warnProxies flags every server-worthy host whose ProxyJump/ProxyCommand
// could not be mapped to a via: (spec S5): it would be dialed directly and
// surface later as an unexplained unreachable. A mapped chain is carried by
// the manifest and warns nothing.
func warnProxies(w *importWarnings, hosts []sshcfg.Host, routes reconcile.Routes) {
	for _, h := range hosts {
		if !serverWorthy(h) || h.Proxy == "" {
			continue
		}
		if why, bad := routes.NotMapped(h.Alias); bad {
			w.addAll(importWarnProxy, fmt.Sprintf("host %q: %s not mapped to via: (%s) — dialed directly; add via: by hand", h.Alias, h.Proxy, why))
		}
	}
}
```


and add:

```go
// manifestServers is the --merge manifest's servers (nil for a fresh
// import): the hops a ProxyJump may name besides the converted hosts.
func manifestServers(mf *manifest.File) []config.Server {
	if mf == nil {
		return nil
	}
	return mf.Config().Servers
}
```

```go
// TestForwards: may a key line open a direct-tcpip channel to host:port
// (the forwarding a via: dependent needs through its bastion).
func TestForwards(t *testing.T) {

		{"permitopen any port", []string{`permitopen="10.0.1.5:*"`}, "10.0.1.5", true},
		{"permitopen any host", []string{`permitopen="*:22"`}, "10.0.1.5", true},
		{"one of two permitopen clauses", []string{`permitopen="10.0.1.6:22"`, `permitopen="10.0.1.5:22"`}, "10.0.1.5", true},
		{"a bracketed IPv6 permitopen", []string{`permitopen="[2001:db8::5]:22"`}, "2001:db8::5", true},
		{"hosts compare literally", []string{`permitopen="WEB.example.com:22"`}, "web.example.com", false},
```
